<!-- ty-source-item:start key=requirement-selected-current-resource-miniapp kind=requirement -->
The current Sky Canvas resource is the explicitly selected implementation constraint for miniapp; DESIGN.md remains the exact token owner and production Source remains the data, permission and business-rule owner.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-development-consumption-boundary-miniapp kind=control -->
Development must consume the complete selected Source closure and structured handoff, map fixture states to the existing production stores and ports, preserve truthful loading/partial/stale/error/permission states, and independently verify the real route.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-01 kind=control -->
family.map-search: Search overlay remains anchored to the field and closes on true focus departure. Data binding: formal spot search/suggestion port. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/pages/map/index.tsx. States: idle, focused, suggestions, query-committed, no-results, error.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-02 kind=control -->
family.quick-filter-chip: Quick conditions commit immediately and the first selection may open Finder peek. Data binding: one shared filter truth. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/components/filter-sheet.tsx. States: unselected, selected, pressed, disabled.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-03 kind=control -->
family.map-analysis-bar-layer: Open on the same physical map with one optional analysis overlay and one selected local time. Data binding: map-coupled observation analysis projection. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/components/source-lift-focus-layer.tsx. States: none, light, total-cloud, tonight-condition, partial, stale, error.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-04 kind=control -->
family.finder-sheet: Handle owns drag, tap and accessibility toggle; no visible expand button, repeated heading or repeated quick filters. Data binding: query/filter/result/favorite selection store. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/components/filter-sheet.tsx. States: closed, peek, expanded, advanced-draft, discarded, committed.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-05 kind=control -->
family.spot-marker-callout: The whole compact card opens detail; no textual view-judgment row. Data binding: formal spot marker and shared selected spot. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/components/spot-card.tsx. States: hidden, selected, stale-summary, unavailable.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-06 kind=control -->
family.spot-result-row: Rows first select the map marker/callout; partition scroll remains operable with hidden scrollbar chrome. Data binding: Finder formal-spot projection. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/components/spot-card.tsx. States: default, selected, favorited, partial, stale, error.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-07 kind=control -->
family.spot-header-actions: Keep the night entry once in the upper hierarchy and navigation as quiet trailing text. Data binding: formal spot identity and route safety. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/features/spot/spot-detail-page.tsx. States: default, route-checking, route-blocked, route-handoff.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-08 kind=control -->
family.favorite-star-ritual: Main star remains dominant; active satellites and short tails stay subordinate and static after settling. Data binding: authenticated favorite relation. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/components/selected-card-star.tsx. States: inactive, activating, active, deactivating, commit-failed, reduced-motion.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-09 kind=control -->
family.astronomy-summary: Conclusion, usable window, source and completeness precede professional evidence. Data binding: versioned astronomy projection for the committed spot/time. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/features/sky/spot-sky-page.tsx. States: loading, ready, partial, stale, unavailable.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-10 kind=control -->
family.astronomy-professional-matrix: Icons assist scanning while aligned tracks and matrices remain the value owner. Data binding: aligned astronomical and observing-condition bands. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/features/sky/spot-sky-page.tsx. States: collapsed, expanded, partial, stale.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-11 kind=control -->
family.sky-time-scrubber: Preview is continuous; commit occurs once and all astronomy sections update from the same context. Data binding: single Observation Context time. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/features/sky/spot-sky-page.tsx. States: idle, previewing, committed, restored.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-12 kind=control -->
family.orientation-sky: Sensor follows phone direction only; there is no manual heading, direction stepper or drag fallback. Data binding: platform orientation stream plus inherited spot/time sky scene. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/features/sky/spot-sky-page.tsx. States: permission, calibrating, ready, denied, unavailable, stale-sensor.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-13 kind=control -->
family.status-recovery: Keep geometry stable, copy concise and retry/cancel effects explicit. Data binding: resource query status and attributable recovery. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/components/status-panel.tsx. States: initial, loading, ready, empty, partial, stale, error, permission-denied.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-14 kind=control -->
family.primary-navigation: Only Map and My are primary destinations. Data binding: current route tree. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/components/custom-nav.tsx. States: map-active, my-active, pressed.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-15 kind=control -->
family.my-plan-settings: My is a route hub and Settings is the sole observation-mode entry. Data binding: identity-scoped plans/settings/account summary. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/features/my/my-library-page.tsx. States: loading, ready, empty, partial, error.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-16 kind=control -->
family.contribution-intake: Spot Detail may prebind only a formal spot id; My opens general intake. Data binding: authenticated contribution kind. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/content/contribution/index.tsx. States: field-report, correction, new-spot-proposal.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-17 kind=control -->
family.contribution-form: Fields reveal progressively by contribution kind; draft and submit are distinct commits. Data binding: identity-scoped durable draft and form schema. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/content/contribution/index.tsx. States: draft, validating, invalid, saving, saved, submitting.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-18 kind=control -->
family.upload-recovery: Retry is idempotent and abandoned media cleanup remains visible and attributable. Data binding: upload session and sanitized derivative receipt. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/content/contribution/index.tsx. States: queued, uploading, paused, failed, retrying, complete, expired.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-19 kind=control -->
family.review-history: Acceptance never implies canonical merge or formal publication. Data binding: submission, merge and publication-impact axes. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/content/contribution/index.tsx. States: draft, pending-review, changes-requested, accepted, rejected, withdrawn, merged, superseded, published-impact.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-miniapp-20 kind=control -->
family.semantic-icon: All icons pass through one mode-aware adapter with accessible labels. Data binding: semantic subject and display mode. Production owner candidate: apps/wechat-miniapp/src/components/semantic-asset.tsx. States: day, night, observation, missing-asset.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->
